- **verify**: Run the project's **type-check and test suite as one gate** and return a single keep/revert verdict for an edit. This is the correctness step of the optimization loop: a type-check proves an edit *compiles*, but it cannot see a swapped operator, an off-by-one, or a dropped branch — those type-check fine and change behaviour. Running the tests alongside `tsc` is what turns *"still compiles"* into * ([jeromeetienne/codespine](https://github.com/jeromeetienne/codespine/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/verify.md) · ★7)
